## Sunspots/sandbox/recipes

### From Unofficial Stationeers Wiki

##### < [User:Sunspots](/User:Sunspots "User:Sunspots") | [sandbox](/User:Sunspots/sandbox "User:Sunspots/sandbox")

Generated using the default recipe files and the official English translations. Not sure if we want to keep article names in sync with English translation to any extent, or I'll build a list of PrefabName>Article Name that could be used for generation.
